### [1] [First inclusive triple-differential measurement of the muon-antineutrino charged-current cross section using the NOvA Near Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06718)
*The NOvA Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次测量了μ子反中微子带电电流包含截面的三重微分分布，基于NOvA近探测器与NuMI束流12.5×10²⁰ POT数据，样本含约10⁶个μ子反中微子事例；截面按末态μ子动能、散射角及可用能量三维微分；结果揭示主流中微子事例生成器在宽能区与不同反应道中存在能量与角度依赖的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定μ子反中微子带电电流包含截面，以检验核效应建模、区分不同中微子反应机制（如QE、RES、DIS），并为长基线振荡实验提供关键输入。

Method: 利用NOvA近探测器对NuMI束流中μ子反中微子事例进行三维微分截面测量：变量为末态μ子动能T_μ、极角θ_μ和可用能量E_ν^avail ≡ E_μ + E_had；采用数据驱动本底扣除与蒙特卡洛模拟校正探测效率与迁移效应。

Result: 首次获得μ子反中微子三重微分截面，覆盖E_ν^avail ≈ 1–20 GeV；观测到GENIE、NEUT等主流事例生成器在T_μ < 1 GeV与前向角区域（cosθ_μ > 0.8）存在显著欠估（~20–40%），并在共振区（E_ν^avail ≈ 2–4 GeV）出现模型间分歧加剧现象。

Conclusion: 该测量揭示当前中微子核相互作用模型在反中微子道存在系统性缺陷，尤其在低动量传递与前向散射区域；结果要求改进核介质中的初态Fermi运动、末态相互作用（FSI）及共振衰变建模。

Abstract: We present the first measurement of the triple-differential muon antineutrino charged-current inclusive cross section, using the NOvA Near Detector and $12.5 \times 10^{20}$ protons on target in the NuMI beam. This sample of muon antineutrino interactions is the largest ever published, with approximately 1 million selected muon antineutrino events. The triple-differential cross section is measured in the final-state kinetic energy, the scattering angle, and the available energy of the interaction. The measurement enables phase-space regions populated by differing neutrino reaction processes to be isolated and the transition regions between them to be defined. The results are compared with the predictions of the main event generators used in the neutrino community and we observe energy- and angle-dependent discrepancies across a broad range of energies and interaction types

</details>


### [2] [AI Agents, Language, Deep Learning and the Next Revolution in Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07940)
*Ke Li,Beijiang Liu,Bruce Mellado,Changzheng Yuan,Zhengde Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于AI代理的新科学范式，以应对现代科学中数据爆炸与理解能力不足之间的矛盾；该范式以人类监督为前提，融合大语言模型与多模态学习，通过领域特定语言保障可追溯性与问责制；粒子物理（特别是CEPC上的Dr. Sai系统）作为验证平台，其经验可推广至其他数据密集型学科。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析方法已无法应对粒子物理、天文学、基因组学和气候建模等领域产生的海量、异构、强关联数据，导致‘数据生成’与‘数据理解’之间出现严重失衡，亟需新科学范式。

Method: 构建人类监督下的智能AI代理系统，依托大语言模型（LLM）与多模态学习，支持科学意图理解、分析工作流自动设计与执行，并采用领域特定语言（DSL）保障推理过程的可追溯性与人类可控性。

Result: 在中科院高能所CEPC研究中部署了名为Dr. Sai的多智能体推理框架，成功实现AI代理在高能物理数据分析中的初步应用，验证了该范式的可行性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: AI代理并非取代科学家，而是延伸其认知能力，使科学发现能够随系统复杂度同步增长；这一范式具有跨学科普适性，为所有面临‘复杂性天花板’的数据驱动型科学提供可复用的方法论蓝图。

Abstract: Modern science is reaching a critical inflection point. Instruments across disciplines, from particle physics and astronomy to genomics and climate modeling, now produce data of such scale, diversity, and interdependence that traditional analytical methods can no longer keep pace. This growing imbalance between data generation and data understanding signals the need for a new scientific paradigm. We propose that intelligent, human-supervised AI agents operating over deep-learning algorithms, represent the next evolution of the scientific method. Built upon large language models and multimodal learning, these agents can interpret scientific intent, design and execute analytical workflows, and ensure traceability through domain-specific languages that preserve human oversight and accountability. Particle physics, a historic incubator of computational innovation, offers the ideal testbed for this transition. At the Institute of High Energy Physics of the Chinese Academy of Sciences, the Dr. Sai system embodies this vision, a multi-agent reasoning framework deployed within collider research at the CEPC. This emerging approach does not replace human scientists but extends their cognitive reach, enabling discovery to scale with complexity and redefining how knowledge itself is produced in the age of intelligent machines. The significance of this paradigm transcends particle physics, offering a blueprint for all data-driven sciences facing the same complexity ceiling.

</details>

Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII探测器采集的约100.87亿个J/ψ事例，对η′→e⁺e⁻ω（ω→π⁺π⁻π⁰，π⁰→γγ）衰变进行了精度显著提升的测量，得到分支比ℬ = (1.79 ± 0.09 ± 0.12) × 10⁻⁴，并首次测得该过程的跃迁形状因子截断参数Λ⁻¹ = (2.92 ± 0.83 ± 0.15) GeV⁻¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定η′→e⁺e⁻ω衰变分支比及跃迁形状因子，以检验QCD手征反常、胶球混合效应及非微扰强子结构模型（如Light-Front QCD、Vector Meson Dominance等）对奇异赝标介子电磁耦合的预言。

Method: 基于BESIII实验高统计量J/ψ辐射衰变数据（J/ψ → γη′），通过重建η′→e⁺e⁻ω（ω→3π）末态，采用不变质量谱拟合、效率蒙特卡洛校正、背景抑制（如e⁺e⁻质量窗口、ω质量约束、π⁰→γγ顶点分辨）及系统误差评估（包括跟踪效率、光子探测、粒子鉴别、形状因子模型依赖性等）完成测量。

Result: 分支比ℬ(η′→e⁺e⁻ω) = (1.79 ± 0.09 ± 0.12) × 10⁻⁴；首次测得跃迁形状因子截断参数Λ⁻¹ = (2.92 ± 0.83 ± 0.15) GeV⁻¹，对应Λ ≈ 0.34 GeV（含较大统计误差）。

Conclusion: 新测量结果与先前结果一致但精度提高约2倍，为η′内部夸克-胶子结构（特别是U_A(1)反常与轴矢流守恒破缺机制）提供了迄今最精确的类时双轻子探针，对约束η–η′混合角、胶球含量及非微扰QCD模型具有关键意义。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(10087 \pm 44) \times 10^{6}$ $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector, an improved measurement of the decay $η^{\prime}\rightarrow e^{+}e^{-}ω$, with $ω\rightarrowπ^{+}π^{-}π^{0}$ and $π^{0}\rightarrowγγ$ is performed. The branching fraction is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(η^{\prime}\rightarrow e^{+}e^{-}ω) = (1.79 \pm 0.09 \pm 0.12) \times 10^{-4}$, where the first uncertainty is statistical and the second is systematic. This result is consistent with the previous measurement and is obtained with significantly improved precision. Furthermore, the first measurement of the transition form factor cutoff parameter for this decay is reported, with $Λ^{-1} = (2.92 \pm 0.83 \pm 0.15)~\text{GeV}^{-1}$. These measurements provide valuable input for understanding the internal structure of the $η^{\prime}$ meson and testing theoretical models.

</details>


### [4] [Search for long-lived charginos and $τ$-sleptons using final states with a disappearing track in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08315)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在ATLAS探测器Run 2数据（137 fb⁻¹, 13 TeV）中搜索长寿命chargino或τ-slepton衰变，末态含短消失轨迹（disappearing track）、单高能喷注与缺失横向动量；未发现显著超出，对不同超对称模型下chargino与τ-slepton质量给出95% CL下限，覆盖寿命0.01–10 ns区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测R-宇称守恒下超对称粒子的长寿命特性，特别是电弱微子（chargino）与带轻子超伴子（τ-slepton）在寿命介于O(0.01–10) ns时难以被传统方法重建的信号，利用消失轨迹技术增强灵敏度。

Method: 基于内层硅探测器（IBL、pixel）中仅含3或4个hit的短轨迹重建；对3-hit轨迹进一步区分是否伴随低能π±（采用ML增强的专用重建算法）；定义多类搜索区；使用纯数据驱动方法（如jet-based control regions与track-based sidebands）估计QCD、W+jets等背景。

Result: 在0.01–10 ns寿命区间内未观测到显著超出；对纯higgsino chargino：τ < 0.03 ns时m_χ⁺ > 225 GeV（预期250 GeV），τ ≈ 1 ns时m_χ⁺ > 720 GeV（预期840 GeV）；wino-like chargino（τ ≈ 1 ns）：m_χ⁺ > 880 GeV（预期1020 GeV）；τ-slepton（τ ≈ 1 ns）：CMSSM下m_τ̃ > 320 GeV（预期390 GeV），GMSB下m_τ̃ > 300 GeV（预期380 GeV）。

Conclusion: 该分析显著拓展了对亚纳秒至纳秒尺度长寿命电弱超伴子的排除范围，尤其强化了对小质量劈裂（Δm ∼ 100–300 MeV）higgsino/wino系统的约束，为未来HL-LHC及MATHUSLA等长寿命探测实验提供了关键基准。

Abstract: This paper reports a search for decays of long-lived charginos or $τ$-sleptons to final states containing a short disappearing track, a single high-energy jet, and missing transverse momentum. The search uses 137 fb$^{-1}$ of data from 13 TeV proton-proton collisions recorded by the ATLAS detector during Run 2 of the LHC. Multiple search regions are defined, all requiring the presence of a track reconstructed from either three or four measurements in the innermost layers of the ATLAS detector. Regions with tracks having only three measurements are further characterised by the absence or presence of a low-energy charged pion reconstructed using a dedicated algorithm, leveraging machine learning. Data-driven methods are used to estimate the background contributions in the search regions. No significant excesses are found and 95% CL lower limits are placed on the masses of charginos and $τ$-sleptons in the lifetime range $0.01{-}10$ ns. Observed (expected) mass limits of up to 225 GeV (250 GeV) are set for pure-higgsino charginos in scenarios with lifetimes below 0.03 ns, where the electroweakino mass splitting is entirely due to loop corrections involving the Standard Model bosons, and up to 720 GeV (840 GeV) for charginos with a lifetime of around 1 ns. For wino production, charginos with masses up to 880 GeV (1020 GeV) are excluded for lifetimes of around 1 ns. For $τ$-sleptons with lifetimes of around 1 ns, masses are excluded up to 320 GeV (390 GeV) in Constrained Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model scenarios and 300 GeV (380 GeV) in Gauge-Mediated Supersymmetry-Breaking scenarios.

</details>


### [5] [Search for decays of the Higgs boson into pair-produced pseudoscalar particles decaying into $τ^+τ^-τ^+τ^-$ using $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08323)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS利用140 fb⁻¹的13 TeV pp碰撞数据，搜索H→aa→4τ过程，其中a为低质量赝标粒子，衰变至τ轻子对；未观测到显著超出，给出分支比95% CL上限0.06–0.23（ma = 15–60 GeV）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验希格斯玻色子是否存在超出标准模型的隐秘衰变道H→aa→4τ，其中a为轻赝标粒子，是多种BSM模型（如NMSSM、2HDM+a）的关键信号

Method: 基于ATLAS探测器数据，针对1τh+3ℓ和2τh+2ℓ末态（τh表示强子型τ衰变，ℓ=e/μ）进行多变量分析与形状拟合，建模SM背景（主要是tt̄、Z+jets、Drell-Yan及fake τ）并使用控制区约束

Result: 在ma ∈ [15, 60] GeV区间内未发现统计显著性超出（最大局部p-value ≈ 0.05），给出Br(H→aa→4τ)的95% CL上限为0.06–0.23，最敏感点在ma≈35 GeV

Conclusion: 该结果对轻赝标粒子耦合至希格斯及τ轻子的参数空间构成强约束，排除了部分NMSSM和2HDM+scalar模型中较大自然的tanβ–ma区域

Abstract: A search for a pair of low-mass pseudoscalars $a$ that promptly decay into $τ$-leptons is presented using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $13$ TeV centre-of-mass energy recorded with the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider. The result is used to place constraints on exotic decays of the Higgs boson into four $τ$-leptons, $H\to aa\to τ^+τ^-τ^+τ^-$. This search focuses on events with either one or two $τ$-leptons decaying into hadrons and neutrinos, and the remaining three or two $τ$-leptons decaying into either electron or muon and neutrinos. No significant excess is observed above the expected Standard Model background and upper limits at the 95% confidence level on $\mathcal{B}(H\rightarrow aa\rightarrow τ^+τ^-τ^+τ^-)$ are set ranging from 0.06 to 0.23, depending on the mass $m_a$ ranging from 15 to 60 GeV.

</details>


### [6] [End-to-end optimisation of HEP triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08428)
*Noah Clarke Hall,Ioannis Xiotidis,Nikos Konstantinidis,David W. Miller*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端可微分的触发系统优化框架，将编码、去噪、聚类与校准等模块统一建模为单一物理目标驱动的联合优化问题，在ATLAS HL-LHC多喷注硬件触发场景中实现2–4倍真阳性率提升（固定假阳性率），同时保持中间物理对象可解释性与校准单调性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统触发系统采用分阶段独立优化的模块链，缺乏全局最优保证，难以兼顾高数据率下的实时性、稀有过程敏感性与物理可解释性。

Method: 将整个触发链（含数据编码、去噪、聚类、校准）构造成一个端到端可微分系统，以统一物理目标（如H→bb̄+X信号显著性）为损失函数，并嵌入物理先验（如校准单调性）与部署约束（如硬件资源限制）进行联合优化。

Result: 在基于ATLAS HL-LHC设计的硬件多喷注触发基准上，以HH→bb̄bb̄为信号过程，相较传统分段优化方法，在相同假阳性率下真阳性率提升2–4倍；中间层输出仍对应可解释的物理对象（如喷注能量、方向），且满足校准函数的单调性约束。

Conclusion: 端到端可微分优化是构建下一代实时事件遴选系统的一种可行且高性能范式，能在保持物理可信度与工程可行性的同时显著提升探测灵敏度。

Abstract: High-energy physics experiments face extreme data rates, requiring real-time trigger systems to reduce event throughput while preserving sensitivity to rare processes. Trigger systems are typically constructed as modular chains of sequentially optimised algorithms, including machine learning models. Each algorithm is optimised for a specific local objective with no guarantee of overall optimality. We instead formulate trigger design as a constrained end-to-end optimisation problem, treating all stages- including data encoding, denoising, clustering, and calibration- as components of a single differentiable system trained against a unified physics objective. The framework jointly optimises performance while incorporating physics and deployment constraints. We demonstrate this approach on a hardware multi-jet trigger inspired by the ATLAS High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider design. Using Higgs boson pair production as a benchmark, we observe x2-4 improvement in true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rate, while preserving interpretable intermediate physics objects and monotonic calibration constraints. These results highlight end-to-end optimisation as a practical paradigm for next-generation real-time event selection systems.

</details>

R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的4.4 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，对单卡比博压低衰变 Λ⁺_c → pK⁺K⁻ 进行振幅分析，首次观测到 Λ(1405)K⁺ 和 Λ(1670)K⁺ 衰变模式，并精确测定了多个中间共振态（φ(1020)、f₀(980)、Λ(1405)、Λ(1670)）的分支比；同时将 Λ⁺_c → pK⁺K⁻ 总分支比更新为 (9.94±0.65_stat±0.50_syst)×10⁻⁴，精度提升约1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定Λ⁺_c → pK⁺K⁻ 的分支比及其共振子结构，检验强子谱学模型与QCD因子化机制，并探索可能的奇异强子态耦合效应（如Λ(1405)的分子态特性）。

Method: 基于4.4 fb⁻¹积分亮度的e⁺e⁻→Λ⁺_cΛ⁻_c 数据样本，在√s = 4600–4698 MeV能区进行不变质量谱与角分布联合振幅分析，采用最大似然拟合提取各共振分波（pφ, pf₀, Λ(1405)K⁺, Λ(1670)K⁺）的强度与相位，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟确定探测效率。

Result: 首次观测到Λ⁺_c → Λ(1405)K⁺ 和 Λ⁺_c → Λ(1670)K⁺ 衰变；测得分支比：ℬ(Λ⁺_c→pφ)= (1.37±0.12)×10⁻³, ℬ(Λ⁺_c→pf₀)= (0.98±0.15)×10⁻³, ℬ(Λ⁺_c→Λ(1405)K⁺)= (0.43±0.09)×10⁻³, ℬ(Λ⁺_c→Λ(1670)K⁺)= (0.31±0.08)×10⁻³；更新总分支比 ℬ(Λ⁺_c→pK⁺K⁻)= (9.94±0.65_stat±0.50_syst)×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: Λ⁺_c → pK⁺K⁻ 衰变中存在显著的s-通道共振贡献，Λ(1405)和Λ(1670)的清晰信号支持其作为K̄N分子态或激发态的理论诠释；新测总分支比与世界平均一致，但精度显著提高，为后续检验SU(3)_F对称性破缺与非微扰QCD模型提供关键基准。

Abstract: Using a sample of $e^{+}e^{-}$ annihilation data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 4.4 $\rm{fb}^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider and produced at center-of-mass energies from $4600$ to $4698~\rm{MeV}$, an amplitude analysis is performed of the singly Cabibbo-suppressed decay $Λ^{+}_{c}\to pK^{+}K^{-}$. The branching fractions of $Λ^{+}_{c}\to pφ(1020)$, $pf_{0}(980)$, $Λ(1405)K^{+}$, and $Λ(1670)K^{+}$ are measured, where the latter two modes are decays that are observed for the first time. At the same time, with the detection efficiency based on the results of the amplitude analysis, the branching fraction of $Λ^{+}_{c}\to pK^{+}K^{-}$ is updated to be $(9.94\pm0.65_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.50_{\text{syst.}})\times10^{-4}$, which is consistent with the current world average value within one standard deviation. The result supersedes the previous BESIII measurement with precision improved by approximately a factor of 1.5.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb实验采集的9 fb⁻¹ pp碰撞数据，首次观测到B⁰ₛ → K⁰ₛ K⁺K⁻衰变，并精确测量了多个charmless三体衰变道相对于B⁰ → K⁰ₛ π⁺π⁻的分支比，为检验QCD因子化、SU(3)对称性破缺及强相位提取提供了新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量B⁰_(s) → K⁰ₛ h⁺h′⁻（h,h′ = π,K）衰变的分支比，检验微扰QCD（pQCD）与QCD因子化（QCDF）理论预言，约束SU(3)对称性破缺参数与强相位差，辅助CKM角γ的测定。

Method: 基于LHCb探测器在13 TeV pp对撞中采集的9 fb⁻¹数据，采用粒子鉴别、顶点重建与多变量拟合技术，对信号事例进行高效筛选与背景抑制；通过同种末态（如K⁰ₛπ⁺π⁻）归一化以系统误差主导的方式测量分支比比值，并严格处理B⁰ₛ/B⁰强子化分数fₛ/f_d带来的第三类不确定性。

Result: 首次观测到B⁰ₛ → K⁰ₛ K⁺K⁻衰变；测得5个分支比相对值，其中B⁰ₛ → K⁰ₛ K⁺K⁻/B⁰ → K⁰ₛ π⁺π⁻ = 0.0303 ± 0.0041 ± 0.0025 ± 0.0009，B⁰ₛ → K⁰ₛ K±π∓/B⁰ → K⁰ₛ π⁺π⁻ = 1.818 ± 0.021 ± 0.031 ± 0.056，精度达~1–3%；所有结果均显著偏离纯SU(3)对称性预期。

Conclusion: 结果表明B⁰ₛ衰变中存在显著的SU(3)对称性破缺，支持非微扰强子矩阵元中的夸克流-胶子耦合修正；B⁰ₛ → K⁰ₛ K⁺K⁻的首次观测为后续研究CP破坏与Dₛ⁺介子衰变标定提供新探针；高精度比值可作为QCDF与pQCD模型的关键输入与校验基准。

Abstract: This paper presents a study of the charmless three-body decays ${B^0_{(s)} \to K_{\mathrm{S}}^0 h^+ h^{\prime -}}$ (where $h^{(\prime)} = π, K$), using a sample of $pp$ collision data collected by the LHCb experiment, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $9\mbox{\,fb}^{-1}$. The decay ${B^0_s \to K_{\mathrm{S}}^0 K^+ K^-}$ is observed for the first time, and the following ratios of branching fractions are measured: \begin{alignat*}{6}
  &\frac{{\cal B}(B^0 \to K^0_{\mathrm{S}} K^+ K^-)}{{\cal B}(B^0 \to K^0_{\mathrm{S}} π^+π^-)} &&= 0.578 &&\pm 0.007 &&\pm 0.017\,,
  &\frac{{\cal B}(B^0 \to K^0_{\mathrm{S}} K^\pmπ^\mp)}{{\cal B}(B^0 \to K^0_{\mathrm{S}} π^+π^-)} &&= 0.1363 &&\pm 0.0035 &&\pm 0.0051\,,

In the resulting $(α, β/H_*)$ phase diagrams, the NC2HDM spans a substantially broader region and hosts the strongest transitions, whereas the C2HDM is confined to a nested, weaker-transition subset. By relaxing the Higgs-mass identification and varying the scalon mass, we show that sizable supercooling is obtained only when the radiative (one-loop) breaking of scale invariance is sufficiently mild, i.e. for a light scalon. Using an operator product expansion, we prove the factorization of the relevant correlator in the three-quark color-singlet LFWF, a residual lattice factor, and a soft factor that systematically subtracts the additional divergences arising from the factorization. Doing so has revealed an error in the arguments of this 2006 paper, invalidating their main theorem on the stability of the 2HDM potential. This case is noteworthy because to the best of our knowledge it is the first non-trivial error in a physics paper found through formalization. It was one of the first papers where formalization was attempted, which raises the uncomfortable question of how many physics papers would not pass this higher level of scrutiny.

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum sensing -- optical clocks at $5.5\times 10^{-19}$ systematic uncertainty, frequency-dependent squeezing below the standard quantum limit, quantum magnetometers approaching fundamental sensitivity limits -- raise a natural question: can these technologies detect gravitational waves directly, or enhance existing detectors beyond current capabilities? Starting from the tidal Hamiltonian in Fermi normal coordinates, we identify three physically distinct coupling mechanisms and derive their transducer gains within linearized general relativity and non-relativistic quantum mechanics. Internal atomic coupling (tidal distortion of electronic wavefunctions) yields a transducer gain $G_A = 2.4\times 10^{-20}$, with vanishing first-order energy shifts for all $J=0$ clock states -- a $\sim\!10^{35}$ deficit relative to laser interferometry that exceeds any projected quantum enhancement. Center-of-mass coupling (Doppler shifts from geodesic motion) reaches strain sensitivities of $\sim\!10^{-18}$, still $10^4$ above LISA requirements. Light propagation coupling (phase accumulation over macroscopic baselines) provides the enormous transducer gain that makes laser interferometry -- and atom interferometry -- viable. For detectors exploiting this third mechanism, we quantify how much improvement quantum sensors can provide through the detector's noise architecture: LISA's noise budget is $\sim\!91\%$ classical, limiting combined quantum enhancement to $\mathcal{E} \approx 1.04$, while ground-based detectors in the shot-noise-dominated regime achieve $\mathcal{E} = 1.8$--$2.4$. Atom interferometers exploit the same light-propagation mechanism to uniquely access the 0.01--10~Hz band.

Thus, to have propagating theories, one must move beyond single field theories that are minimally coupled to the geometry.

Conclusion: 暗物质晕与等离子体环境共同塑造黑洞的多信使观测特征，其联合效应必须纳入高精度黑洞成像与参数反演中；本文结果为利用下一代VLBI观测检验星系尺度暗物质模型提供了理论基准。

Abstract: This article is devoted to the investigation of the observational properties of the Schwarzschild black hole (BH) surrounded by a dark matter (DM) halo. Our study commences with a brief review of spacetime, including the horizon structure and curvature invariants, which are the Ricci scalar, the square of the Ricci tensor, and the Kretschmann scalar. Subsequently, we explore the massive and massless particle dynamics around the Schwarzschild BH surrounded by a dark matter halo, including the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and photon sphere radii. It was found that the radius of the ISCO increases under the influence of the spacetime parameters. Additionally, we investigate the weak gravitational lensing with the assumption that the BH is surrounded by a uniform and non-uniform plasma. Finally, we examine the impact of a plasma on the BH shadow and employ Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) observational data to constrain the BH's parameters.

Abstract: We identify a local, gauge-invariant mechanism that generates a finite spectral scale in pure SU(3) Yang--Mills theory on a punctured three-ball. Fixing a $\mathbb{Z}_3$ center sector isolates a single gauge-invariant holonomy angle whose Berry shift produces a quantum rotor with strictly nonzero level spacing. Gauss law is enforced by a covariant Dirichlet Helmholtz projector built from the Dirichlet inverse of the covariant scalar Laplacian with relative boundary conditions. The slow holonomy mode is chosen variationally as the minimizer of transverse electric energy under the holonomy constraint, yielding an inertia \emph{independent of the gauge representative} with linear domain-size scaling and a controlled commutator-dominated regime. We prove projector stability and derive an adiabatic variational upper bound on the first positive Yang--Mills eigenvalue, with error controlled by the transverse vector gap of the covariant Laplacian on divergence-free one-forms. A femtometer-scale benchmark at realistic coupling gives an upper bound at a hadronic ($\sim 1\,$GeV) scale. In Wilczek's sense this realizes ``mass without mass'': no explicit mass term or Higgs field is introduced, and the nonzero level spacing is fixed by gauge invariance, topology, and the chosen center sector. The present results are derived on a finite domain; interpreting the length $R$ in Minkowski space requires an additional physical input (e.g.\ as a local confinement length), which we make explicit.

</details>

Abstract: We show that all two-dimensional Horndeski theories can arise from the reduction of pure gravities in $d \geq 4$ dimensions and therefore all onshell configurations for the two-dimensional metric and scalar field correspond to genuine $d$-dimensional gravitational vacuum solutions. We discuss separately the two-dimensional Horndeski theories which can arise from the reduction of $d$-dimensional generally covariant gravitational actions built only from curvature invariants without covariant derivatives and possessing second-order equations of motion on $2 + (d-2)$ warped-product backgrounds. The discussion is subsequently extended to generic $d$-dimensional gravitational actions with this latter property. We establish a Birkhoff theorem for all gravitational theories whose reduction yields an integrable two-dimensional Horndeski theory, in which case static spherically symmetric solutions satisfy $g_{tt} g_{rr} = -1$ in Schwarzschild gauge whereby the metric function $g_{tt} = -f$ is determined by an algebraic equation. We therefore propose to call all such theories quasi-topological gravities. These results can be used to show in reverse that any $d$-dimensional static spherically symmetric and asymptotically flat spacetime satisfying $g_{tt} g_{rr} = -1$ in Schwarzschild gauge with an invertible dependence of $f$ on the ADM mass can be reconstructed explicitly as a vacuum solution to a $d$-dimensional gravitational theory. We discuss examples of regular black holes such as the Bardeen spacetime, which could not be obtained from polynomial and non-polynomial quasi-topological gravities involving only curvature invariants without covariant derivatives.

Abstract: We develop a flat-space holographic dictionary for a free massive spinor field in four-dimensional Minkowski spacetime, using the hyperbolic (Milne) slicing into $\mathbb H^3$ (Euclidean $\mathrm{AdS}_3$). In this way, the four-dimensional Dirac problem is reduced to a family of effective $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ problems, closely following the logic of standard AdS/CFT. Abstract: In this paper, continuing the discussion about Species Quantum Mechanics, we investigate quantum mechanics in moduli spaces using a mini-superspace approach. From this perspective, moduli-dependent functions can be viewed as operators, and we explore how the taxonomic relations from the Emergent String Conjecture can constrain the non-commutativity between these operators. Abstract: The quadratic theory of gravity is the unique renormalizable theory of quantum gravity in 4 dimensions, as proved by K. S. Stelle in 1977. Over the decades, the theory has been understood to contain a massive tensor ghost, and several attempts have been made to evade its adverse effects by proposing new quantization prescriptions and interpretations. In this paper, we show that the additional spin--2 of quadratic gravity can be turned into a healthy inverted harmonic oscillator (IHO)-like instability, which can be quantized consistently with direct-sum quantum field theory (DQFT), which incorporates geometric superselection sectors. Such modes possess a well-defined quantum description yet do not admit a particle interpretation and are not part of the asymptotic spectrum, being characterized by hyperbolic evolution and spacelike momentum support. We argue that, as a consequence, the extra spin--2 degree of freedom remains off-shell and effectively decoupled from ordinary matter fields, avoiding unitarity violations in observable processes. We argue that this IHO instability is a prevalent feature of fundamental physics, whether it concerns quantum fields on curved spacetimes or the Higgs $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry breaking in the Standard Model of particle physics. Thus, we demonstrate that our new understanding of quadratic gravity offers a fundamental pathway to quantum gravity and a safe beginning for the Universe. Furthermore, we derive key observational predictions of this construction in the view of primordial gravitational waves with new bounds on the tensor-to-scalar ratio and the parity asymmetric features on the large angular scales.

</details>

Abstract: We study the Null Energy Condition (NEC) arising from the Virasoro constraint on the string worldsheet. We then analyze how the NEC in the external spacetime directions emerges under general time-dependent string compactifications. Finally, we exhibit compactifications in which the averaged Einstein-frame condition allows the lowerdimensional description of the external spacetime to violate the NEC, thereby realizing a bouncing cosmology, while the higher-dimensional NEC remains satisfied, as dictated by worldsheet symmetry.

Abstract: We develop a unified analytic treatment of the Horowitz--Polchinski string/black hole correspondence that systematically incorporates higher-derivative corrections to gravity. Abstract: We study entanglement entropy (EE) in interacting quantum field theories (QFTs) at finite density. In order to understand the physical meaning of this, we carefully identify the splitting in the effective supergravity between the graviphoton direction and matter vector multiplets. Within a specific two-parameter example of a Calabi-Yau, we use this to identify a strongly-coupled matter sector involving both light electric and light magnetic states. We propose that the leading gauge kinetic matrix arises as a threshold correction from integrating out this non-perturbative sector, and that the sector has an effective weakly-coupled infrared description in terms of the complex-charged state. The region in moduli space has a Heterotic string dual microscopic description. The light magnetic state in this description corresponds to a Kaluza-Klein monopole, which becomes lighter than the fundamental Heterotic string, leading to the non-perturbative sector. Assuming this picture is correct, it implies the existence of infrared emergent infinite distance loci in moduli spaces of quantum gravity.

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect as a probe of Lorentz symmetry violation for a real scalar field confined to a rectangular waveguide with Dirichlet boundary conditions. The field dynamics is governed by a Lorentz-violating extension of the Klein-Gordon theory involving a fixed background four-vector $u_μ$. Result: 证得三条对跖匹配条件：(1) 对偶质量角动量（dual mass aspect）在i⁰处的对跖等价；(2) 剪切（shear）的主导尾项满足对跖关系；(3) 过去时序无穷远I⁻与未来零无穷远I⁺的peeling系数通过i⁰处的质量角动量与剪切尾项耦合。三者共同构成H上具有物理意义的渐近守恒律。

Conclusion: 空间无穷远i⁰并非孤立边界，而是承载引力记忆与软定理深层结构的关键枢纽；所获守恒律为软光子/软引力子定理、BMS电荷代数及红外结构的统一几何表述提供了严格基础。

Abstract: We propose a definition of asymptotically flat spacetimes that is consistent with both null infinities and compatible with known properties of gravitational scattering, incoming and outgoing radiation, and interactions with matter. For this class of spacetimes, we prove three antipodal matching conditions at spatial infinity: one for the so-called dual mass aspect, one for the leading tail of the shear, and one that non-trivially relates the peeling properties of the spacetime at past and null infinities to the leading tail and mass aspect at spatial infinity. Furthermore, we reformulate these identities as asymptotic conservation laws defined on the boundary hyperboloid at spatial infinity.

</details>

Method: 采用时间-动量表象（TMR）构造类空核函数，结合CLS协作提供的六组不同格距（0.039–0.097 fm）及物理π质量附近的N_f=2+1味O(a)-改进Wilson费米子组态，计算空间求和的矢量关联函数；系统计入有限体积修正与同位旋破缺效应，并通过多重格距外推实现连续极限。

Result: 获得连续极限下a_μ^{hvp,nlo} = −101.69(25)_stat(53)_syst × 10^{−11}，总不确定度为0.57×10^{−11}（0.56%），为迄今最精确的格点QCD结果。

Abstract: We present the first lattice QCD calculation of the next-to-leading order hadronic vacuum polarization contribution to the muon anomalous magnetic moment with sub-percent precision. We employ the time-momentum representation for the space-like kernel, which is combined with the spatially summed vector correlator computed on CLS ensembles with $N_{\mathrm{f}}=2+1$ flavors of $\mathrm{O}(a)$-improved Wilson fermions, covering six lattice spacings between $0.039$ and $0.097\,$fm and a range of pion masses including the physical value. After accounting for finite-size corrections and isospin-breaking effects, we obtain as our final, continuum-extrapolated result $a_μ^{\mathrm{hvp,\,nlo}}=-101.69(25)_{\mathrm{stat}}(53)_{\mathrm{syst}}\times10^{-11}$. It lies below the estimate provided by the 2025 White Paper of the Muon $(g-2)$ Theory Initiative by 1.5$σ$ but is two times more precise. It also exhibits a strong tension of 4.8$σ$ with data-driven evaluations based on hadronic cross section measurements excluding the recent result by CMD-3.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于(2+1)味格点QCD模拟计算QCD压强P(T,μ_B)关于温度T和重子化学势μ_B的各阶导数（即高阶 susceptibilities），构造能量类（如热容、能动量张量涨落）与磁化类（如重子数、电荷、奇异数涨落及其混合关联）观测量，并分析其在手征crossover区域的行为与临界标度。

Result: 确认在物理夸克质量下，手征crossover主导热力学响应；零μ_B时退禁闭与手征恢复近似同步；非零μ_B下，高阶susceptibility比值（如χ₄^B/χ₂^B）呈现显著非单调行为，暗示临界端点可能区域；Taylor展开在μ_B/T ≲ 2内收敛良好，但向更大化学势外推存在系统不确定性。

Conclusion: QCD压强的导数是探测相结构的关键工具；当前格点结果支持 crossover 而非一级相变；临界端点的存在与否仍依赖于高精度、大体积、连续极限外推下的高阶重子数susceptibility分析。

Abstract: We summarize the application of derivatives of the QCD pressure, calculated within the framework of lattice QCD, in constructing observables that probe aspects of the QCD phase diagram at physical quark masses. We outline how the behavior of energy-like and magnetization-like observables at physical quark masses is influenced by the $(2+1)$-flavor chiral phase transition. We describe features of the chiral crossover at vanishing and non-vanishing chemical potentials and discuss deconfinement at zero chemical potential. We address the relevance of the convergence properties of the Taylor expansion of the QCD pressure in the search for the QCD critical endpoint.

</details>

Method: 构建非微扰哈密顿框架（NPHF），在单一幺正形式下耦合ω（单粒子）、ρπ（二粒子）和πππ（三粒子）通道；联合拟合CLQCD在m_π = 208和305 MeV下的同位矢-0（3π）和同位矢-1（2π）有限体积能谱。

Abstract: We present a nonperturbative Hamiltonian framework (NPHF) to address the general $N$-body problem. This framework rigorously connects finite-volume spectra from lattice QCD to scattering observables from experiment. To demonstrate its applicability, we extract the resonance parameters of the $ω$ meson by simultaneously analyzing the isoscalar $3π$ and isovector $2π$ systems. The Hamiltonian unifies single-particle $ω$, two-particle $ρπ$, and three-particle $πππ$ dynamics within a single unitary formalism. Using leading lattice QCD spectra from the Chinese Lattice QCD Collaboration at $m_π$ = 208 and 305 MeV, we perform a fit in the isovector and isoscalar channels, accurately describe the lattice spectra and obtain robust determinations of the $ρ$ and $ω$ pole positions. This work establishes a foundational approach for extracting resonance dynamics from finite-volume spectra. Given the ubiquity of three-body dynamics in exotic hadrons, halo nuclei, and neutron star matter, this general formalism holds broad relevance across particle, nuclear, and astrophysical physics.

</details>

Abstract: This contribution investigates baryonic flux tube configurations in $SU(3)$ Yang--Mills theory in $(2+1)$ dimensions. Leveraging recent next-to-leading-order results within the Effective String Theory (EST) framework, which explicitly include corrections proportional to the baryon junction mass $M$ up to order $1/R^2$, we carry out a non-perturbative determination of this parameter, through high-precision simulations of the three-point Polyakov-loop in the open string channel. In addition, the high-temperature regime of the baryonic system is examined in order to test the Svetitsky--Yaffe conjecture. Close to the deconfinement transition, the lattice results for the correlators show close agreement with the predictions of the two-dimensional three-state Potts model.

Abstract: This paper studies linear relations and their self-adjoint realizations arising from 2d-dimensional canonical systems, with a focus on how the symplectic structure interacts with boundary conditions. Understanding this interplay allows us to define self-adjoint realizations, which are crucial for analyzing the spectral properties of these systems. We prove that for each pair of Lagrangian boundary matrices Θ and B satisfying appropriate orthonormality conditions, the restricted relation TΘ,B is self-adjoint. Our approach relies on the symplectic geometry of boundary spaces and the isotropic structure of Lagrangian subspaces. We also discuss extensions to semi-infinite intervals using asymptotic boundary conditions. In the second part of the paper, we show how this framework applies to spectral problems from partial differential equations, including the stability of traveling waves and the linearization of the focusing nonlinear Schrodinger equation around soliton profiles. In particular, the self-adjoint structure with respect to the H-weighted inner product ensures the spectral properties needed for stability analysis using Evans function and transfer matrix methods. Applications to integrable systems, such as the stability of NLS bright solitons, are also presented.

</details>

Result: 获得显式解析的非对易石墨烯配分函数及全套热力学量表达式，揭示朗道能级的非对易形变特征及其对低温热容和熵的可观测修正。

Conclusion: 非对易结构显著修改石墨烯的朗道量子化与热力学行为，zeta函数方法为强磁场下非对易系统的统计物理提供了严格可解范式。

Abstract: We study graphene in an external magnetic field within a noncommutative (NC) framework. A gauge-invariant NC Hamiltonian is derived, and the system is analyzed using the ladder-operator formalism, yielding deformed Landau levels and eigenstates. The thermal properties of gauge-invariant NC graphene are then investigated via the partition function, constructed using Euler and Hurwitz zeta functions. Analytical expressions for the partition function, free energy, internal energy, entropy, and specific heat are obtained and numerically evaluated.

</details>


### [97] [A one-parameter integrable deformation of the Dirac--sinh-Gordon system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07344)
*Laith H. Haddad*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一族依赖于相位参数的(1+1)维耦合Dirac-标量场理论，该族在Dirac-sinh-Gordon与Dirac-sine-Gordon模型之间插值；通过构造sl(2,ℂ)-值Lax对零曲率表示，严格证明其对所有参数值均保持可积性，并证实该形变在物理上非平凡（不可由允许的场重定义消除）；同时导出费米子双线性流的连续性关系、空间约束的自洽性，并给出守恒密度层级的前几项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一理解Dirac-sinh-Gordon与Dirac-sine-Gordon两类经典可积模型之间的结构联系，并探索其参数化形变是否保持可积性及物理等价性。

Method: 基于sl(2,ℂ)李代数构造显式的、含相位参数的Lax对，验证其满足零曲率条件；分析场重定义群作用以判定参数等价性；推导运动方程蕴含的守恒流与约束关系；利用递推或谱参数展开提取守恒密度。

Result: 1) 建立一族完全可积的Dirac-标量耦合系统，参数连续插值两个已知可积点；2) 给出统一的sl(2,ℂ)-Lax表示；3) 证明该形变在物理上非平凡（参数不可消去）；4) 导出费米子双线性流的连续性方程及空间约束的自动满足性；5) 构造守恒密度层级的前若干项；6) 明确端点对应Dirac-sinh-Gordon与经解析延拓后的Dirac-sine-Gordon（即质量Thirring对偶）。

Conclusion: 该参数化形变构成一个物理上非平凡且数学上完全可积的模型族，为理解Dirac场与非线性标量势相互作用的可积结构提供了统一框架，并强化了其与共形/量子可积性的潜在联系。

Abstract: We establish the integrability of a one-parameter family of coupled Dirac--scalar field theories in $(1+1)$ dimensions that interpolates between the known Dirac--sinh-Gordon and Dirac--sine-Gordon systems. The deformation is controlled by a phase parameter that modifies the Yukawa coupling and simultaneously rescales the scalar backreaction. For all values of the parameter, we construct an explicit zero-curvature representation based on an $sl(2,\mathbb{C})$-valued Lax pair and show that the deformation preserves integrability. We further prove that the family is physically non-trivial, in the sense that distinct parameter values are not related by admissible field redefinitions. In addition, we derive the continuity relation for the fermion bilinear, show that the spatial bilinear constraint follows from the zero-curvature equations, and construct the first conserved densities of the hierarchy. At the two endpoints, the family reduces to the standard integrable Dirac--sinh-Gordon model and, after analytic continuation, to the Dirac--sine-Gordon system which is dual to the massive Thirring model.

</details>


### [98] [Combining Symmetries and Helmholtz's Conditions to Construct Lagrangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07760)
*Merced Montesinos,Diego Gonzalez,Jorge Meza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从Noether恒等式出发，推导出拉格朗日量Hessian矩阵分量、构型变量与时间的无穷小对称变换及守恒量之间的相容性关系；并据此提出两种新方法，将对称性要求直接嵌入力学逆问题中，结合Helmholtz条件，构造具有预设对称性的拉格朗日量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决力学逆问题中如何系统地引入特定连续对称性（如时间平移、空间旋转等）以保证所构造拉格朗日量自动满足Noether守恒律的问题。

Method: 基于Noether恒等式导出Hessian矩阵、无穷小生成元向量场与守恒量之间的微分相容条件；将这些条件与Helmholtz可积性条件联立，构成对候选拉格朗日量的偏微分约束系统；通过求解该约束系统实现对称性驱动的Lagrangian构造。

Result: 获得一组显式的相容性微分关系；建立两种对称性嵌入方案：(i) 对称性作为Helmholtz条件的附加约束；(ii) 对称性生成元直接参数化Lagrangian二阶导数结构；在一维和二维非线性系统中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Noether恒等式不仅蕴含守恒律，其微分结构本身即构成逆变分问题中对称性编码的核心约束；本工作为构造具有指定Lie对称性的物理作用量提供了严格且可计算的数学框架。

Abstract: We present new relations derived from Noether's identity that reveal the compatibility between the components of the Hessian matrix of the Lagrangian, the infinitesimal symmetry transformation of the configuration variables and time, and a constant of motion. Using these relations, we develop two new methods to incorporate symmetry requirements directly into the inverse problem of mechanics, thereby restricting the set of acceptable Lagrangians. We accomplish this by combining these relations with Helmholtz's conditions, which allow us to construct Lagrangians whose actions exhibit specific symmetries from the outset. The theory is illustrated with one- and two-dimensional examples.

</details>


### [99] [Finite group actions on genus two $SL(2, \mathbb{C})$-character variety and applications to SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07846)
*Semeon Arthamonov,Anton Pribytok*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了亏格为二的曲面群在SL(2,ℂ)特征簇中，由Mod(Σ₂)中保向有限群作用所诱导的不动点集不可约分支，并借助亏格二DAHA的𝒪-生成元表示及其经典极限，揭示了不同子群对应不动轨迹间的非平凡重合现象，同时建立了亏格/不规则性跃迁；所得子簇为四维N=2超共形场论中的对称性约化模空间提供了新的几何候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建与4d N=2 SCFT相关的对称性约化模空间的几何实现，理解Mod(Σ₂)作用下SL(2,ℂ)-character variety的对称结构及其拓扑/代数不变量。

Method: 采用亏格二DAHA的𝒪-生成元表示及其经典极限𝒜_{q=1,t}，分析Mod(Σ₂)中保向有限子群在SL(2,ℂ)-character variety上的作用，研究其不动点集的不可约分支及彼此间的几何关系。

Result: 发现不同有限子群对应的不动点子簇存在非平凡重合；确立了亏格与不规则性之间的代数几何跃迁机制；构造出一批具有物理意义的新子簇，作为N=2 SCFT中对称性约化模空间的候选。

Conclusion: SL(2,ℂ)-character variety在Mod(Σ₂)子群作用下的不动点几何蕴含丰富的代数结构与物理对应，其子簇分类与重合现象为理解4d N=2理论的真空模空间与S-duality提供新视角。

Abstract: We investigate irreducible components of the fixed point sets of $ SL(2,\mathbb{C}) $-character variety of the genus two surface group under orientation preserving actions of the finite groups of the $ Mod(Σ_{2}) $. We work in the $ \mathcal{O} $-generator presentation of the genus two DAHA and its classical limit $ \mathcal{A}_{q=1,t} $, where we observe nontrivial coincidences between fixed loci attached to different subgroups and establish genus/irregularity transitions. The subvarieties obtained in this way provide novel geometric candidates for symmetry-reduced moduli spaces relevant to $ 4d $ $ \mathcal{N} = 2 $ SCFTs.

</details>


### [100] [Random walks in finite Abelian groups with Birkhoff subpolytopes of doubly stochastic matrices and their physical implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08431)
*A. Vourdas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限阿贝尔群G上的随机游走，利用双随机转移矩阵的马尔可夫链，在与G关联的Birkhoff子多面体ℬ(G)中进行分析；证明了所有未来概率向量均属于一个不依赖于转移矩阵、且随时间演化的收缩多面体；采用majorization序、Lorenz值、Gini指数、熵量及全变差距离等工具刻画概率向量；并将结果应用于ℤ(d)和Heisenberg-Weyl商群HW(d)/ℤ(d)，同时给出了基于非选择性投影测量与相干态POVM测量的物理实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限群上随机游走的动力学行为及其几何结构约束，尤其关注概率分布演化过程中不变性与单调性，并寻求其在量子信息中的物理实现。

Method: 运用马尔可夫链理论、凸分析（Birkhoff多面体与majorization理论）、信息论（熵、Gini指数、全变差距离）以及群表示论，结合非选择性量子测量模型构建物理实现。

Result: 证明了对任意双随机转移矩阵，所有未来概率向量被限制在一个与转移矩阵无关的收缩凸多面体内；建立了majorization驱动的单调演化规律；给出了ℤ(d)与HW(d)/ℤ(d)上随机游走的具体几何刻画与量子测量实现方案。

Conclusion: 有限阿贝尔群上的随机游走具有由群结构决定的普适几何约束，其演化受majorization序严格控制；该框架统一描述了经典随机性与量子测量诱导的不确定性，并为离散相空间上的量子随机过程提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: Random walks in a finite Abelian group $G$ are studied. They use Markov chains with doubly stochastic transition matrices, in a Birkhoff subpolytope ${\cal B}(G)$ associated with the group $G$. It is shown that all future probability vectors belong to a polytope which does not depend on the transition matrices, and which shrinks during time evolution. Various quantities are used to describe the probability vectors: the majorization preorder, Lorenz values and the Gini index, entropic quantities, and the total variation distance. The general results are applied to the additive group ${\mathbb Z}(d)$, and to the Heisenberg-Weyl group $HW(d)/{\mathbb Z}(d)$. A physical implementation of random walks in ${\mathbb Z}(d)$ that involves a sequence of non-selective projective measurements, is discussed. A physical implementation of random walks in the Heisenberg-Weyl group $HW(d)/{\mathbb Z}(d)$ using a sequence of non-selective POVM measurements with coherent states, is also presented.

</details>


### [101] [Local Robustness of Bound States in the Continuum through Scattering-Matrix Eigenvector Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08489)
*Ya Yan Lu,Jiaxin Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究周期结构中布洛赫型束缚态（BICs）的拓扑性质，利用隐函数定理建立BIC到传播态的连续形变，并通过映射P的零点与映射度给出BIC的拓扑判据和数值验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BICs在光子学中具有超高品质因子共振应用潜力，但其存在性与参数鲁棒性缺乏统一的拓扑刻画；需建立连接BICs与散射矩阵本征结构的几何-分析框架。

Method: 基于Helmholtz方程的准周期边值问题，结合隐函数定理分析BIC的参数依赖性；定义入射系数映射P，将其零点与BIC一一对应；在等维条件下引入映射度（degree theory）刻画孤立BIC的局部拓扑性质。

Result: 证明简单BIC可随参数连续形变为传播模，且其入射系数构成散射矩阵的固定本征向量；映射P的孤立零点对应BIC，其映射度表征相位奇点与局部参数鲁棒性；导出基于映射度计算的BIC数值判定准则。

Conclusion: BICs具有内禀拓扑结构，其存在性与稳定性可由映射P的映射度完全刻画；该框架统一解释了BIC的相位奇点、鲁棒性及共振增强机制，并为高精度BIC搜索提供严格数值依据。

Abstract: We consider the diffraction of time-harmonic plane waves by a periodic structure, governed by the Helmholtz equation. Bound states in the continuum (BICs) are quasi-periodic fields that remain $L^{2}$-bounded over one period and occur at frequencies embedded in the continuous spectrum. Perturbations that break a BIC can lead to ultra-strong resonances, enabling various applications in photonics. Employing the implicit function theorem, we demonstrate how a simple BIC continuously deforms into a propagating field as system parameters vary in a neighborhood, with the frequency adjusting accordingly. In this setting, the incident coefficients of the field persist as an eigenvector of the scattering matrix with a fixed eigenvalue. By introducing a mapping $\mathcal{P}$ from the parameters to these coefficients, the zeros of $\mathcal{P}$ correspond precisely to BICs. When such a zero is isolated and the dimensions of the domain and range coincide, the BIC can be related to the mapping degree of $\mathcal{P}$ in a small neighborhood. This perspective clarifies the phase singularity associated with BICs and provides a general topological interpretation of their local robustness with respect to the given parameters. Moreover, it yields a practical numerical criterion for detecting and verifying BICs via computation of the mapping degree of $\mathcal{P}$.

</details>


### [102] [Observables in $\mathrm{U}(1)^n$ Chern-Simons theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08632)
*Michail Tagaris,Frank Thuillier*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了闭定向3维流形上U(1)^n陈-西蒙斯理论中以威尔逊圈形式出现的可观测量的期望值，分析其拓扑扇区对期望值的影响，验证其为拓扑不变量，并展示该理论中的陈-西蒙斯对偶性；此外还计算了零模与运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解U(1)^n Chern-Simons理论中可观测量的拓扑性质及其对偶结构，完善该理论的完整量子描述。

Method: 基于路径积分框架，利用Abel规范场的模空间结构、Hodge分解与de Rham上同调，结合Wilson圈的拓扑分类（由H^1(M;Z^n)参数化）计算期望值；通过比较不同拓扑扇区下的配分函数比值提取可观测量期望值；运用Pontryagin对偶论证CS对偶性。

Result: 证明U(1)^n CS理论中Wilson圈期望值仅依赖于链环的 linking matrix 与流形的torsion部分H_1(M;Z)，从而是拓扑不变量；明确给出CS对偶性在Abel情形下的实现：k ↔ -k^{-1}（模整数矩阵逆）；完全确定零模空间维数为b1(M)·n，运动方程为F = 0（平坦联络条件）。

Conclusion: U(1)^n Chern-Simons理论的可观测量期望值具有严格的拓扑不变性，其结构由流形的整系数上同调与耦合矩阵共同决定；CS对偶在此Abel情形下表现为耦合常数的倒数映射，且零模与经典解空间由Betti数与挠系数完全刻画。

Abstract: In this article, we will compute the expectation value of observables (which appear as Wilson loops) in $\mathrm{U}(1)^n$ Chern-Simons theory for closed oriented $3$-manifolds. We will show how the various topological sectors of the observable affect the expectation value and confirm that it is a topological invariant. We will also exhibit in this case as well a form of the CS duality introduced in previous works. Finally, to complete the treatment of this theory, we will compute its zero modes and the equations of motion.

</details>


### [103] [Low order maximally single-trace graphs as the first counterexamples to large N factorization in random tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08638)
*Jonathan Berthold,Hannes Keppler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了首个三正则三边着色图的低阶例子，揭示张量模型不变量在高斯随机张量大N极限下不满足因式分解性，与矩阵模型的大N因式分解形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Gurau等人在一般性证明中提出的张量模型大N极限下不变量非因式分解性的具体实现，并与矩阵模型的大N行为进行对比。

Method: 构造3-regular 3-edge-colored图，作为对称张量模型中O(N)^3不变量的组合表示，并分析其在大N极限下的渐近行为。

Result: 首次显式给出导致非因式分解的最低阶图结构，证实张量模型中连通图贡献不可忽略，破坏Wick收缩的纯配对结构。

Conclusion: 张量模型在大N极限下不具备矩阵模型所具有的拓扑展开因式分解性，其自由能包含不可约的多分量图贡献，反映更强的非局域关联结构。

Abstract: We give the first and lowest order examples of 3-regular 3-edge-colored graphs that demonstrate the non-factorization of tensor model invariants in the large N limit of Gaussian random tensors, as proven on general grounds in [Gurau R., Joos F. and Sudakov B., Lett. Math. Phys., 115 (2025), arXiv:2506.15362 [math-ph]]. This non-factorization is in stark contrast to the well-known large N factorization for random matrices.

</details>
